# Shared CJK 306 Source-Link Register

Status: broader discussion/replacement research artifact. The current V1 is the 126-character ZDIC-complete selection in [`zdic-v1-complete-manifest.json`](../../content/research/zdic-v1-complete-manifest.json). This register is a manual-review index, not a license clearance or specialist approval.

Date: 2026-09-03

## Purpose

This register gives one review row for each of the 306 shared identities that have an EVOBC index entry in all six stages. It is intended to let the project owner compare source quality before we select or generate any museum artwork.

The shared identity is the product key. The EVOBC record may use a Simplified or Traditional label, so the `EVOBC IDs/forms` column preserves the exact source label. The `stage evidence` column gives the number of indexed images, the EVOBC source IDs present, and one representative filename per source ID. The full set of filenames is available through the linked metadata file and the downloadable archive.

## How to open the evidence

1. Open the [EVOBC Data-EN archive](https://huggingface.co/datasets/HaisuGuan/EVOBC/resolve/main/Data-EN.zip). The archive is about 5.1 GB; it is not bundled into this project.
2. Use the exact path `Data/EVOBC/<ID>/<filename>` described in the [EVOBC repository README](https://github.com/RomanticGodVAN/character-Evolution-Dataset#dataset-structure). The ID and representative filenames are in each row below.
3. Use the [EVOBC metadata file](https://github.com/RomanticGodVAN/character-Evolution-Dataset/blob/main/List_of_EVOBC.json?plain=1) to search the ID and inspect every filename, not only the representative examples.
4. For an independent Oracle Bone comparison, open [Xiaoxuetang’s Oracle Bone database](https://xiaoxue.iis.sinica.edu.tw/jiaguwen), enter the character in the `字形` field, and submit. Its form posts to `/jiaguwen/PageResult/PageResult`; this is why the register links the stable database entry page and records the exact character rather than inventing a GET query URL.
5. Where present, the `Dong SVG` links point to the [Hanzi/Kanji Etymology Dictionary’s Dong Chinese SVGs](https://github.com/lbm364dl/hanzi-etymology-dict/tree/main/docs/glyphs/dong_chinese). These are a separate source family and currently cover Oracle, Bronze, and Seal SVGs only—not all six EVOBC stages.

## Important limits

- An EVOBC index row proves that the dataset metadata names an image; it does not by itself prove that the interpretation is uncontested, that the image is suitable for our UI, or that we can redistribute it.
- `src0`–`src7` are the numeric source identifiers preserved by EVOBC. They are not treated as quality rankings. We will rate actual image clarity, cropping, legibility, provenance, and license separately.
- A missing `Dong SVG` link means that this particular supplementary repository did not contain that stage file. It does not mean that no historical form exists elsewhere.
- Generated teaching illustrations remain separate from historical glyph evidence and must never replace a missing historical form.

## Stage key

`OBC` = Oracle Bone; `BI` = Bronze Inscriptions; `SAC` = Spring and Autumn; `WSC` = Warring States; `SS` = Seal Script; `CS` = Clerical Script.

## Research coverage

This pass checked 11 source pages across approximately 9 source families/web domains. They do not all serve the same purpose:

| Source family | Per-character precision in this register | Use |
|---|---|---|
| EVOBC metadata + Data-EN archive | Exact ID, stage, count, source ID, and filename references for all 306 | Primary six-stage image inventory |
| Dong Chinese SVG repository | Exact direct SVG links where a matching Oracle/Bronze/Seal file exists | Independent visual comparison |
| Xiaoxuetang / Academia Sinica | Exact character to enter in the database’s `字形` field | Independent Oracle Bone lookup |
| HUST-OBC | Independent dataset/paper reference; no invented per-character mapping | Cross-check of Oracle Bone corpus availability |
| TCS 808, Aige, Kangxi | Corpus/universe references | Four-language eligibility boundary |
| Unicode and broader scholarly summaries | Historical/encoding context | Background only |

The 11 pages establish the research path and the 306 index pool. They do not mean that every one of the 306 has already been visually adjudicated across every source. That is the next review activity: open the archive/database references, choose the clearest historically faithful form per stage, record the reviewer decision, and only then mark an asset as accepted.

## Same-symbol comparison example: 光

`光` is EVOBC record `01073`. These links all refer to the same character, but they do not provide identical stage sets:

- [EVOBC metadata: search `01073` and `光`](https://github.com/RomanticGodVAN/character-Evolution-Dataset/blob/main/List_of_EVOBC.json?plain=1), then open the six-stage files from [Data-EN.zip](https://huggingface.co/datasets/HaisuGuan/EVOBC/resolve/main/Data-EN.zip) under `Data/EVOBC/01073/`.
- [Academia Sinica CharDB page for 光](https://chardb.iis.sinica.edu.tw/evolution.jsp?cid=7485) — page-level comparison for Oracle Bone, Bronze, Chu manuscripts, and Small Seal. Its page identifies the Oracle reference as `合集94正` and Bronze as `集成05417`.
- [Academia Sinica CharDB Oracle image for 光](http://char.iis.sinica.edu.tw/TTF/getTTF1.aspx?word=%E5%85%89&f=%E7%94%B2%E9%AA%A8%E6%96%87&s=200&c=black) and [Bronze image](http://char.iis.sinica.edu.tw/TTF/getTTF1.aspx?word=%E5%85%89&f=%E9%87%91%E6%96%87&s=200&c=black) — direct image endpoints extracted from that page.
- [漢典 / ZDIC page for 光](https://zdic.net/hans/%E5%85%89) — shows Oracle Bone, Bronze, Chu manuscripts, Small Seal, Qin slips, Clerical, and modern comparison material; it also lists multiple image/catalogue entries.
- [Wiktionary page for 光](https://zh.wiktionary.org/zh-hans/%E5%85%89) — independent reference with ancient-form panels and links to the underlying historical-form sources.
- [Xiaoxuetang Oracle Bone database](https://xiaoxue.iis.sinica.edu.tw/jiaguwen) — enter `光` in the `字形` field for an independent Oracle Bone search.
- [Dong Chinese Oracle SVG for 光](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/lbm364dl/hanzi-etymology-dict/main/docs/glyphs/dong_chinese/%E5%85%89/%E5%85%89_oracle.svg) and [Dong Chinese Seal SVG for 光](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/lbm364dl/hanzi-etymology-dict/main/docs/glyphs/dong_chinese/%E5%85%89/%E5%85%89_seal.svg) — direct downloadable vector comparisons. This repository does not currently provide a matching local Bronze SVG for `光`.

`光` is therefore a good comparison object: EVOBC indexes all six stages, while independent sites expose different subsets and catalogues. That difference is exactly why the final museum chain needs a source-by-source asset decision rather than assuming one website’s sequence is complete or definitive.

### Rough coverage against the finalized historical stops

For source comparison, the denominator is now the four historical glyph stages: Oracle Bone, Bronze, Small Seal, and Clerical. Origin is our own educational illustration, and Regular/Modern is a separate overview layer; neither is included in historical-source coverage.

| Source | Directly provides | Rough journey coverage |
|---|---|---:|
| EVOBC | Oracle, Bronze, Small Seal, Clerical; plus SAC/WSC research stages | 4/4 |
| Academia Sinica CharDB | Oracle, Bronze, Chu manuscripts, Small Seal | 3/4 |
| 漢典 / ZDIC | Oracle, Bronze, Chu manuscripts, Small Seal, Qin slips, Clerical | 4/4 |
| Wiktionary | Oracle, Bronze, Chu manuscripts, Small Seal | roughly 3/4 |
| Dong Chinese SVG repository | For `光`, direct Oracle and Small Seal SVGs | 2/4 direct historical assets |
| Xiaoxuetang | Oracle Bone database lookup | 1/4 |
| HangulHanja | Modern Hanja meaning/etymology | 0/4 historical assets |

These numbers count source-accessible historical stages, not historical legitimacy or image quality. `Origin / Illustration` and Regular/Modern are intentionally excluded because they are separate product layers.
